D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{0}}(\rho)

In this section, we show how
D_{\mathrm{LT},\sigma_{0}}(\rho)
and
D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{0}}(\rho)
are related. Let
\mathbb{C}_{p}
be the
p
-adic completion of the algebraic closure
\overline{K}
of
K
, and for any perfectoid field
M
, let
M^{\flat}
be the tilt of
M
(
[
Sch12
, Lemma 3.4]
). Let
\widehat{K_{\infty}}
(resp.
\widehat{K(\mu_{p^{\infty}})}
) be the
p
-adic completion of
K_{\infty}
(resp.
K(\mu_{p^{\infty}})
), then
\widehat{K_{\infty}}
and
\widehat{K(\mu_{p^{\infty}})}
are perfectoid fields. By
[
Win83
, 4.2.1.Proposition & 4.3.4.Corollaire]
and
[
Sch12
, Theorem 3.7.(ii)]
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\mathbb{F}_{q}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{p^{-\infty}})\negthinspace)\cong\widehat{K_{\infty}}^{\flat}\cong(\mathbb{C}_{p}^{\flat})^{\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K_{\infty})},\\
\mathbb{F}_{q}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{p^{-\infty}})\negthinspace)\cong\widehat{K(\mu_{p^{\infty}})}^{\flat}\cong(\mathbb{C}_{p}^{\flat})^{\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K(\mu_{p^{\infty}}))}.\end{cases}

(6.2)

These isomorphisms are compatible with the actions of
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)
, where
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)
acts on
\mathbb{F}_{q}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{p^{-\infty}})\negthinspace)
(resp.
\mathbb{F}_{q}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{p^{-\infty}})\negthinspace)
) via
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)\twoheadrightarrow\mathrm{Gal}(K_{\infty}/K)\cong\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}
(resp.
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)\twoheadrightarrow\mathrm{Gal}(K(\mu_{p^{\infty}})/K)\cong\mathbb{Z}_{p}^{\times}
). Thus these isomorphisms are compatible with the actions of
\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}
and of
\mathbb{Z}_{p}^{\times}
respectively. As
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K_{\infty})
is a closed subgroup of
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K(\mu_{p^{\infty}}))
, there is an embedding which commutes with the
\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}
-action:

\displaystyle\mathbb{F}_{q}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}})\negthinspace)\hookrightarrow\mathbb{F}_{q}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{p^{-\infty}})\negthinspace)\xrightarrow{\sim}(\mathbb{C}_{p}^{\flat})^{\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K(\mu_{p^{\infty}}))}\hookrightarrow(\mathbb{C}_{p}^{\flat})^{\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K_{\infty})}\xrightarrow{\sim}\mathbb{F}_{q}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{p^{-\infty}})\negthinspace).

(6.3)

We endow
\left(A_{\mathrm{LT},K}^{\mathrm{ur}}\right)^{\wedge}
(resp.
\left(A_{\mathrm{cycl},K}^{\mathrm{ur}}\right)^{\wedge}
) with the
p
-adic topology, then the action of
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K_{\infty})
(resp.
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K(\mu_{p^{\infty}}))
) on
\left(A_{\mathrm{LT},K}^{\mathrm{ur}}\right)^{\wedge}
(resp.
\left(A_{\mathrm{cycl},K}^{\mathrm{ur}}\right)^{\wedge}
) is continuous.

Lemma 6.3

For
n\geq 1
, we have

(a)

H^{1}_{\mathrm{cont}}\left(\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K_{\infty}),\left(A_{\mathrm{LT},K}^{\mathrm{ur}}\right)^{\wedge}\right)=0
.

(b)

H^{1}_{\mathrm{cont}}\left(\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K_{\infty}),\mathrm{GL}_{n}\left(\left(A_{\mathrm{LT},K}^{\mathrm{ur}}\right)^{\wedge}\right)\right)=0
.

(c)

H^{1}_{\mathrm{cont}}\left(\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K(\mu_{p^{\infty}})),\mathrm{GL}_{n}\left(\left(A_{\mathrm{cycl},K}^{\mathrm{ur}}\right)^{\wedge}\right)\right)=0
.

Proof
.

The same strategy of the proof of Corollary
5.5
applies to (a), and (b) is a direct consequence of (a) and the fact that
H^{1}_{\mathrm{cont}}\left(\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K_{\infty}),\mathrm{GL}_{n}\left(\mathbb{F}_{q}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}})\negthinspace)^{\mathrm{sep}}\right)\right)=0

(Hilbert satz 90, see for example
[
Ser79
, p.151, Proposition 3]
), and the proof of (c) is exactly the same as (b), hence we omit the details.

We define
W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}_{q}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{p^{-\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\coloneq\mathcal{O}_{E}\otimes_{\sigma_{0},\mathcal{O}_{K}}W\left(\mathbb{F}_{q}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{p^{-\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)
. The following result reveals the relation between Fontaine’s cyclotomic
(\varphi_{q},\mathbb{Z}_{p}^{\times})
-modules and Lubin-Tate
(\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-modules.

Corollary 6.4

Let
\rho
be a finite type
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-representation of
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)
. There is a canonical isomorphism of
W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}_{q}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{p^{-\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)
-modules which commutes with the
(\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-actions:

W\left(\mathbb{F}_{q}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{p^{-\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{LT},K}}D_{\mathrm{LT},\sigma_{0}}(\rho)\xrightarrow{\sim}W\left(\mathbb{F}_{q}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{p^{-\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{cycl},K}}D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{0}}(\rho),

(6.4)

where
\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}
acts on
D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{0}}(\rho)
via the norm map
\mathrm{Norm}_{K/\mathbb{Q}_{p}}:\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}\rightarrow\mathbb{Z}_{p}^{\times}
.

Proof
.

First we show that for any finite continuous
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-representation
\rho
of
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)
,
there are natural isomorphisms of
\mathcal{O}_{E}\otimes_{\sigma_{0},\mathcal{O}_{K}}W\left(\mathbb{C}_{p}^{\flat}\right)
-modules commuting with the actions of
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)
:

\displaystyle W\big(\mathbb{C}_{p}^{\flat}\big)\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{LT},K}}D_{\mathrm{LT},\sigma_{0}}(\rho)\xrightarrow{\sim}W\big(\mathbb{C}_{p}^{\flat}\big)\otimes_{\sigma_{0},\mathcal{O}_{K}}\rho\xleftarrow{\sim}W\big(\mathbb{C}_{p}^{\flat}\big)\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{cycl},K}}D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{0}}(\rho).

(6.5)

If
\rho
is a finite free continuous
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-representation of
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)
, we can directly apply Lemma
6.3
and the construction of
D_{\mathrm{LT},\sigma_{0}}
and
D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{0}}
to deduce that the natural homomorphisms

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\left(A_{\mathrm{LT},K}^{\mathrm{ur}}\right)^{\wedge}\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{LT},K}}D_{\mathrm{LT},\sigma_{0}}(\rho)\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\meno}$}}}\mathrel{\mkern-3.0mu}\rightarrow\left(A_{\mathrm{LT},K}^{\mathrm{ur}}\right)^{\wedge}\otimes_{\sigma_{0},\mathcal{O}_{K}}\rho,&\text{as}\ \mathcal{O}_{E}\otimes_{\sigma_{0},\mathcal{O}_{K}}\left(A_{\mathrm{LT},K}^{\mathrm{ur}}\right)^{\wedge}\text{-modules},\\
\left(A_{\mathrm{cycl},K}^{\mathrm{ur}}\right)^{\wedge}\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{cycl},K}}D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{0}}(\rho)\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\meno}$}}}\mathrel{\mkern-3.0mu}\rightarrow\left(A_{\mathrm{cycl},K}^{\mathrm{ur}}\right)^{\wedge}\otimes_{\sigma_{0},\mathcal{O}_{K}}\rho,&\text{as}\ \mathcal{O}_{E}\otimes_{\sigma_{0},\mathcal{O}_{K}}\left(A_{\mathrm{cycl},K}^{\mathrm{ur}}\right)^{\wedge}\text{-modules},\end{cases}

(6.6)

are isomorphisms.
Then (
6.5
) follows.

If
\rho=\bigcup_{n=1}^{\infty}\rho[\varpi^{n}]
is a torsion representation, since
\rho
is finitely generated, there exists
N\geq 1
such that
\varpi^{N}\cdot\rho=0
. We will show that (
6.5
) holds by induction on
N\geq 1
. If
\rho=\rho[\varpi]
, i.e.
\rho
is a finite dimensional continuous
\mathbb{F}
-representation of
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)
, then (
6.5
) follows from the proof of
[
BHHMS23a
, Proposition 2.1.2]
. If
N\geq 2
, as
D_{\mathrm{LT},\sigma_{0}}(-)
and
D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{0}}(-)
are equivalences of categories, we have exact sequences

0\rightarrow D_{\mathrm{LT},\sigma_{0}}(\rho[\varpi^{N-1}])\rightarrow D_{\mathrm{LT},\sigma_{0}}(\rho)\rightarrow D_{\mathrm{LT},\sigma_{0}}(\rho/\rho[\varpi^{N-1}])\rightarrow 0

and

0\rightarrow D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{0}}(\rho[\varpi^{N-1}])\rightarrow D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{0}}(\rho)\rightarrow D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{0}}(\rho/\rho[\varpi^{N-1}])\rightarrow 0.

Since
A_{\mathrm{LT},K}\hookrightarrow W\left(\mathbb{C}_{p}^{\flat}\right)
,
\mathcal{O}_{K}\hookrightarrow W\left(\mathbb{C}_{p}^{\flat}\right)
and
A_{\mathrm{cycl},K}\hookrightarrow W\left(\mathbb{C}_{p}^{\flat}\right)
are flat, there is a commutative of diagram of
\mathcal{O}_{E}\otimes_{\sigma_{0},\mathcal{O}_{K}}W\left(\mathbb{C}_{p}^{\flat}\right)
-modules with exact rows

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces W\left(\mathbb{C}_{p}^{\flat}\right)\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{LT},K}}D_{\mathrm{LT},\sigma_{0}}(\rho[\varpi^{N-1}])}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces W\left(\mathbb{C}_{p}^{\flat}\right)\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{LT},K}}D_{\mathrm{LT},\sigma_{0}}(\rho)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces W\left(\mathbb{C}_{p}^{\flat}\right)\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{LT},K}}D_{\mathrm{LT},\sigma_{0}}(\rho/\rho[\varpi^{N-1}])}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces W\left(\mathbb{C}_{p}^{\flat}\right)\otimes_{\sigma_{0},\mathcal{O}_{K}}\rho[\varpi^{N-1}]}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces W\left(\mathbb{C}_{p}^{\flat}\right)\otimes_{\sigma_{0},\mathcal{O}_{K}}\rho}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces W\left(\mathbb{C}_{p}^{\flat}\right)\otimes_{\sigma_{0},\mathcal{O}_{K}}\left(\rho/\rho[\varpi^{N-1}]\right)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces W\left(\mathbb{C}_{p}^{\flat}\right)\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{cycl},K}}D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{0}}(\rho[\varpi^{N-1}])}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces W\left(\mathbb{C}_{p}^{\flat}\right)\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{cycl},K}}D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{0}}(\rho)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces W\left(\mathbb{C}_{p}^{\flat}\right)\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{cycl},K}}D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{0}}(\rho/\rho[\varpi^{N-1}])}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\wr}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\wr}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\wr}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\wr}

where the vertical maps are induced by (
6.6
). Then (
6.5
) is a direct consequence of the induction hypothesis and the five lemma.

For a general finite
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-representation
\rho
, let
\rho_{\mathrm{tor}}
be the torsion subrepresentation of
\rho
, then there is a short exact sequence

0\rightarrow\rho_{\mathrm{tor}}\rightarrow\rho\rightarrow\rho/\rho_{\mathrm{tor}}\rightarrow 0.

The same arguments as for torsion representations applies here as well, which implies that (
6.5
) holds for any finite
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-representation
\rho
. Then taking
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K_{\infty})
-invariants, the conclusion follows.

Since

W\left(\mathbb{F}_{q}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{p^{-\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{LT},K}}D_{\mathrm{LT},\sigma_{0}}(\rho)\cong W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}_{q}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{p^{-\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{LT},E,\sigma_{0}}}D_{\mathrm{LT},\sigma_{0}}(\rho)

and

W\left(\mathbb{F}_{q}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{p^{-\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{cycl},K}}D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{0}}(\rho)\cong W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}_{q}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{p^{-\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{cycl},E}}D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{0}}(\rho),

the isomorphism (
6.4
) can be rewritten as

W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}_{q}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{p^{-\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{LT},E,\sigma_{0}}}D_{\mathrm{LT},\sigma_{0}}(\rho)\cong W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}_{q}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{p^{-\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{cycl},E}}D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{0}}(\rho).

(6.7)

7
Étale
(\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-modules attached to Galois representations

In this section, we associate étale
(\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-modules to finite type continuous
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-representations and finite dimensional continuous
E
-representations of
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)
, and prove some basic properties of these functors.

7.1
Finitely presented étale
(\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-modules attached to finite type continuous
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-representations of
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)

In this section, we construct the functor
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}:\mathrm{Rep}_{\mathcal{O}_{E}}(\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K))\rightarrow\mathrm{Mod}^{\mathrm{\acute{e}t,fp}}_{\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}}\left(A_{\mathrm{mv},E}\right)
and show the fully faithfulness of
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}
for
0\leq i\leq f-1
.

For
0\leq i\leq f-1
, consider the following diagram of topological rings:

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces W_{E}(A_{\infty})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces W_{E}(A_{\infty}^{\prime})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces A_{\mathrm{LT},E,\sigma_{0}},}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces m}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathrm{pr}_{i}}

(7.1)

where the map
\mathrm{pr}_{i}:A_{\mathrm{LT},E,\sigma_{0}}\hookrightarrow W_{E}(A_{\infty})
is the composition of (
3.8
) and
W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}_{q}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\hookrightarrow W_{E}(A_{\infty}^{\prime}),\ [T_{\mathrm{LT}}]\mapstochar\rightarrow[T_{\mathrm{LT},i}]
.
The map
\mathrm{pr}_{i}
commutes with the continuous
(\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-actions (where
\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}
acts on
W_{E}(A_{\infty}^{\prime})
via the
i
-th inclusion
\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}{\mathrel{\rightarrow}^{j_{i}}}(\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})^{f}
).

For a finite type continuous
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-representation
\rho
of
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)
,
0\leq i\leq f-1
, we put

\displaystyle D_{W_{E}(A_{\infty})}^{(i)}(\rho)\coloneq\left(W_{E}(A_{\infty}^{\prime})\otimes_{\mathrm{pr}_{i},A_{\mathrm{LT},E,\sigma_{0}}}D_{\mathrm{LT},\sigma_{0}}(\rho)\right)^{\Delta_{1}},

(7.2)

which is a finitely presented étale
(\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-module over
W_{E}(A_{\infty})
by Theorem
5.11
. Then we define

\displaystyle D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}\coloneq\mathrm{Dec}\circ D_{W_{E}(A_{\infty})}^{(i)}:\mathrm{Rep}_{\mathcal{O}_{E}}(\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K))

\displaystyle\rightarrow\mathrm{Mod}^{\mathrm{\acute{e}t,fp}}_{\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}}(A_{\mathrm{mv},E})

\displaystyle\rho

\displaystyle\mapstochar\rightarrow\mathrm{Dec}\left(D_{W_{E}(A_{\infty})}^{(i)}(\rho)\right),

where
\mathrm{Dec}
is the decompletion and deperfection functor (Definition
4.15
).
If
\rho
is a finite free
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-representation, then
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(\rho)
is a finite free
A_{\mathrm{mv},E}
-module of the same rank. Moreover, for different
i
, these functors
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}
are related by the following lemma:

Lemma 7.1

For
0\leq i\leq f-1
and any finite type
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-representation
\rho
of
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)
, there is an isomorphism of finitely presented étale
(\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-modules over
A_{\mathrm{mv},E}
which is functorial in
\rho
:

\displaystyle\phi_{i}:A_{\mathrm{mv},E}\otimes_{\varphi,A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(\rho)\xrightarrow{\sim}D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i+1)}(\rho).

(7.3)

Moreover,
\phi_{f-1}\circ\phi_{f-2}\circ\cdots\circ\phi_{0}:A_{\mathrm{mv},E}\otimes_{\varphi_{q},A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho)\rightarrow D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho)
is the linearization map
\mathrm{id}\otimes{\varphi_{q}}
.

Proof
.

Recall that there is an automorphism of
A_{\infty}=\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\sigma_{0}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\left\langle\left(\frac{T_{\sigma_{i}}}{T_{\sigma_{0}}}\right)^{\pm 1/p^{\infty}}:1\leq i\leq f-1\right\rangle

\displaystyle\varphi:A_{\infty}\rightarrow A_{\infty},\ T_{\sigma_{i}}\mapstochar\rightarrow T_{\sigma_{i-1}}^{p},

and an
\mathbb{F}
-linear automorphism of
A_{\infty}^{\prime}=\mathbb{F}\left(\negthinspace\left(T_{\mathrm{LT},0}^{1/p^{\infty}}\right)\negthinspace\right)\left\langle\left(\frac{T_{\mathrm{LT},i}}{T_{\mathrm{LT},0}^{p^{i}}}\right)^{\pm 1/p^{\infty}}:1\leq i\leq f-1\right\rangle
:

\displaystyle\varphi:A_{\infty}^{\prime}\rightarrow A_{\infty}^{\prime},\ T_{\mathrm{LT},i}\mapstochar\rightarrow\begin{cases}T_{\mathrm{LT},i+1},&i\neq f-1\\
T_{\mathrm{LT},0}^{q},&i=f-1.\end{cases}

(See
[
BHHMS23a
,
\lx@sectionsign
2.4 (43),
\lx@sectionsign
2.5 (47)]
). Moreover, the homomorphism
m:A_{\infty}\hookrightarrow A_{\infty}^{\prime}
satisfies
m\circ\varphi=\varphi\circ m
. These automorphisms induce
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-linear automorphisms
\varphi:W_{E}(A_{\infty})\rightarrow W_{E}(A_{\infty})
and
\varphi:W_{E}(A_{\infty}^{\prime})\rightarrow W_{E}(A_{\infty}^{\prime})
. One can easily check that

\displaystyle\varphi\circ\mathrm{pr}_{i}=\begin{cases}\mathrm{pr}_{i+1},&i\neq f-1\\
\mathrm{pr}_{0}\circ\varphi_{q},&i=f-1.\end{cases}

By Corollary
4.19
, it suffices to construct a natural isomorphism of finitely presented étale
(\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-modules

\phi_{i}:W_{E}(A_{\infty})\otimes_{\varphi,W_{E}(A_{\infty})}D_{W_{E}(A_{\infty})}^{(i)}(\rho)\xrightarrow{\sim}D_{W_{E}(A_{\infty})}^{(i+1)}(\rho).

Using the definition
D_{W_{E}(A_{\infty})}^{(i)}(\rho)=\left(W_{E}(A_{\infty}^{\prime})\otimes_{\mathrm{pr}_{i},A_{\mathrm{LT},E,\sigma_{0}}}D_{\mathrm{LT},\sigma_{0}}(\rho)\right)^{\Delta_{1}}
, we can construct
\phi_{i}
as follows:

\displaystyle\phi_{i}:a\otimes(x\otimes v)\mapstochar\rightarrow\begin{cases}a\cdot\varphi(x)\otimes v,&0\leq i\leq f-2,\\
a\cdot\varphi(x)\otimes\varphi_{q}(v),&i=f-1,\end{cases}

where
a\in W_{E}(A_{\infty})
,
x\in W_{E}(A_{\infty}^{\prime})
,
v\in D_{\mathrm{LT},\sigma_{0}}(\rho)
. A direct computation shows that
\phi_{i}
is well-defined for every
i
and commutes with
\varphi_{q}
and the action of
\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}
. Since the composition
\phi_{i+f-1}\circ\phi_{i+f-2}\circ\cdots\circ\phi_{i+1}\circ\phi_{i}
is the linearization map

\mathrm{id}\otimes{\varphi_{q}}:W_{E}(A_{\infty})\otimes_{\varphi_{q},W_{E}(A_{\infty})}D_{W_{E}(A_{\infty})}^{(i)}(\rho)\xrightarrow{\sim}D_{W_{E}(A_{\infty})}^{(i)}(\rho)

which is an isomorphism, we see that
\phi_{i}
is an isomorphism for every
i
.

Since
W_{E}(A_{\infty}^{\prime})
is an integral domain, and
\mathrm{pr}_{i}:A_{\mathrm{LT},E,\sigma_{0}}\rightarrow W_{E}(A_{\infty}^{\prime})
is injective, we see that
W_{E}(A_{\infty}^{\prime})
is torsion-free as an
A_{\mathrm{LT},E,\sigma_{0}}
-module. Since
A_{\mathrm{LT},E,\sigma_{0}}
is a discrete valuation ring, in particular,
A_{\mathrm{LT},E,\sigma_{0}}
is a principal ideal domain and
W_{E}(A_{\infty}^{\prime})
is a flat
A_{\mathrm{LT},E,\sigma_{0}}
-algebra. Moreover, the morphism
\mathrm{Spec}(W_{E}(A_{\infty}^{\prime}))\rightarrow\mathrm{Spec}(A_{\mathrm{LT},E,\sigma_{0}})
is surjective, we conclude that the ring map
A_{\mathrm{LT},E,\sigma_{0}}\xrightarrow{\mathrm{pr}_{i}}W_{E}(A_{\infty}^{\prime})
is faithfully flat, hence
W_{E}(A_{\infty}^{\prime})\otimes_{\mathrm{pr}_{i},A_{\mathrm{LT},E,\sigma_{0}}}-
is faithful and exact. As
(-)^{\Delta_{1}}
and
\mathrm{Dec}
are equivalences of categories, we see that the functor
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}=\mathrm{Dec}\circ\left(W_{E}(A_{\infty}^{\prime})\otimes_{\mathrm{pr}_{i},A_{\mathrm{LT},E,\sigma_{0}}}-\right)^{\Delta_{1}}
is also faithful and exact. We will show that the functor
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}
is full. As in
[
BHHMS23a
,
\lx@sectionsign
2.8]
, the key is to find out how
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}
and
D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{i}}
are related.

Since
K/\mathbb{Q}_{p}
is unramified, the map
\mathcal{O}_{K}\xrightarrow{\mathrm{tr}}\mathbb{Z}_{p}

is a surjective map of
\mathbb{Z}_{p}
-modules. It induces a surjective ring map of Iwasawa algebras
\mathcal{O}_{K}[\negthinspace[\mathcal{O}_{K}]\negthinspace]\xrightarrow{\mathrm{tr}}\mathcal{O}_{K}[\negthinspace[\mathbb{Z}_{p}]\negthinspace]
which commutes with the
(\varphi_{q},\mathbb{Z}_{p}^{\times})
-actions. We choose variables as in (
2.1
) for
\mathcal{O}_{K}[\negthinspace[\mathcal{O}_{K}]\negthinspace]
so that

\mathcal{O}_{K}[\negthinspace[\mathcal{O}_{K}]\negthinspace]=\mathcal{O}_{K}[\negthinspace[T_{0},\dots,T_{f-1}]\negthinspace],

and let
T_{\mathrm{cycl}}\coloneq[1]-1\in\mathcal{O}_{K}[\negthinspace[\mathbb{Z}_{p}]\negthinspace]
, then
\mathcal{O}_{K}[\negthinspace[\mathbb{Z}_{p}]\negthinspace]=\mathcal{O}_{K}[\negthinspace[T_{\mathrm{cycl}}]\negthinspace]
. By the third paragraph of
[
BHHMS23
,
\lx@sectionsign
3.1.3, p.116]
, for
0\leq i\leq f-1
, the map
\mathrm{tr}
satisfies

\mathrm{tr}(T_{i})\equiv-T_{\mathrm{cycl}}\mod(p,T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{2}),

hence it induces a continuous surjection
\mathrm{tr}:A_{\mathrm{mv},E}\twoheadrightarrow A_{\mathrm{cycl},E}
which commutes with the
(\varphi_{q},\mathbb{Z}_{p}^{\times})
-actions.

Similarly, the trace map also induces a surjection
\mathrm{tr}:A_{\infty}\twoheadrightarrow\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)
which commutes with the
(\varphi_{q},\mathbb{Z}_{p}^{\times})
-actions (see
[
BHHMS23
,
\lx@sectionsign
3.1.3, p.116]
). It uniquely lifts to an
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-linear ring homomorphism, also denoted by
\mathrm{tr}:W_{E}(A_{\infty})\twoheadrightarrow W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)
.

Lemma 7.2

There is a commutative diagram

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces A_{\mathrm{cycl},E}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces W_{E}(A_{\infty})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathrm{tr}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathrm{tr}}

(7.4)

where the left vertical maps is (
3.4
)
and the right vertical map is (
3.6
)

Proof
.

Since
\mathrm{tr}:A_{\mathrm{mv},E}\twoheadrightarrow A_{\mathrm{cycl},E}
commutes with
\varphi_{q}
,
it induces a continuous surjection

f:\varinjlim_{\varphi_{q}}A_{\mathrm{mv},E}\twoheadrightarrow\varinjlim_{\varphi_{q}}A_{\mathrm{cycl},E}.

By taking topological completions, we get a ring homomorphism

f:W_{E}(A_{\infty})\cong\left(\varinjlim_{\varphi_{q}}A_{\mathrm{mv},E}\right)^{\wedge}\rightarrow\left(\varinjlim_{\varphi_{q}}A_{\mathrm{cycl},E}\right)^{\wedge}\cong W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)

which makes the following diagram commute:

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces A_{\mathrm{cycl},E}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces W_{E}(A_{\infty})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right).}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathrm{tr}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces f}

Thus it suffices to show that the two maps
f:W_{E}(A_{\infty})\rightarrow W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)
and
\mathrm{tr}:W_{E}(A_{\infty})\rightarrow W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)
are identical. Hence it suffices to verify that the two homomorphisms of strict
p
-rings
f:W(A_{\infty})\rightarrow W\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)
and
\mathrm{tr}:W(A_{\infty})\rightarrow W\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)
are equal.
By the theory of strict
p
-rings (
[
Ser79
, Chapter II,
\lx@sectionsign
5 Proposition 10]
), it suffices to verify that
f
and
\mathrm{tr}
are equal after reducing modulo
p
, which follows straightforwardly.

Proposition 7.3

There exists an embedding
\sigma_{d}\in\{\sigma_{0},\dots,\sigma_{f-1}\}
such that for any finite type
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-representation
\rho
of
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)
, there is a canonical isomorphism of finitely presented étale
(\varphi_{q},\mathbb{Z}_{p}^{\times})
-modules over
A_{\mathrm{cycl},E}
:

A_{\mathrm{cycl},E}\otimes_{\mathrm{tr},A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho)\cong D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{d}}(\rho).

Proof
.

By Lemma
4.20
, it suffices to show that there exists an embedding
\sigma_{d}:\mathcal{O}_{K}\hookrightarrow\mathcal{O}_{E}
such that

W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho)\cong W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{cycl},E}}D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{d}}(\rho).

Using (
7.4
), the left hand side is

W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho)\cong W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{\mathrm{tr},W_{E}(A_{\infty})}D_{W_{E}(A_{\infty})}^{(0)}(\rho).

Hence we need to show that there is an embedding
\sigma_{d}
and a canonical isomorphism of finitely presented étale
(\varphi_{q},\mathbb{Z}_{p}^{\times})
-modules over
W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)
:

\displaystyle W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{\mathrm{tr},W_{E}(A_{\infty})}D_{W_{E}(A_{\infty})}^{(0)}(\rho)\cong W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{cycl},E}}D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{d}}(\rho).

(7.5)

Recall that there is a commutative diagram of perfectoid rings over
\mathbb{F}
(
[
BHHMS23a
,
\lx@sectionsign
2.8 (54)]
) which commutes with the
(\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-action

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces A_{\infty}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces A_{\infty}^{\prime}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathrm{tr}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces m}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\iota}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces T_{\mathrm{LT},i}^{p^{-n}}\mapstochar\rightarrow T_{\mathrm{LT},\sigma_{0}}^{p^{i-n}}}

(7.6)

where the right vertical map is induced by the left vertical map of
[
BHHMS23a
,
\lx@sectionsign
2.8 (53)]
, and
\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}
acts on
\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)
via the norm map
\mathrm{Norm}_{K/\mathbb{Q}_{p}}:\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}\twoheadrightarrow\mathbb{Z}_{p}^{\times}
. Since
\mathrm{Gal}(K_{\infty}/K(\mu_{p^{\infty}}))\cong\mathrm{ker}(\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}\xrightarrow{\mathrm{Norm}_{K/\mathbb{Q}_{p}}}\mathbb{Z}_{p}^{\times})
, the right vertical map
\iota
decomposes as

\iota:\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\rightarrow\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)^{\mathrm{Gal}(K_{\infty}/K(\mu_{p^{\infty}}))}\hookrightarrow\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace).

Recall that tilting functor gives an isomorphism (
6.3
)

\displaystyle\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\cong\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)^{\mathrm{Gal}(K_{\infty}/K(\mu_{p^{\infty}}))},

thus the first part of
\iota
is a continuous endomorphism of
\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)
which commutes with the action of
\mathbb{Z}_{p}^{\times}
. Hence
by
[
BR22
, Theorem 3.1]
, there exists
\gamma\in\mathbb{Q}_{p}^{\times}
such that
\iota
is given by the composition

\displaystyle\iota:\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\xrightarrow[\sim]{T_{\mathrm{cycl}}\mapstochar\rightarrow(1+T_{\mathrm{cycl}})^{\gamma}-1}\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\hookrightarrow\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)

(7.7)

where the last injection is (
6.3
).

The commutative diagram (
7.6
) lifts to a commutative diagram which commutes with the
(\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-actions:

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces W_{E}\left(A_{\infty}\right)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces W_{E}\left(A_{\infty}^{\prime}\right)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathrm{tr}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces m}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\iota}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{[T_{\mathrm{LT},i}]}^{p^{-n}}\mapstochar\rightarrow{[T_{\mathrm{LT},\sigma_{0}}]}^{p^{i-n}}}

(7.8)

As a consequence of (
7.7
), the right vertical map
\iota
decomposes as

\iota:W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\xrightarrow[\sim]{[T_{\mathrm{cycl}}]\mapstochar\rightarrow\left[(1+T_{\mathrm{cycl}})^{\gamma}-1\right]}W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\hookrightarrow W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right),

where
[T_{\mathrm{cycl}}]
and
\left[(1+T_{\mathrm{cycl}})^{\gamma}-1\right]
are the Teichmüller lifts of
T_{\mathrm{cycl}}
and
(1+T_{\mathrm{cycl}})^{\gamma}-1
respectively. We write
\gamma=p^{mf+d}\cdot a
for some
a\in\mathbb{Z}_{p}^{\times}
,
m\in\mathbb{Z}
and
d\in\{0,\dots,f-1\}
, since the
\mathbb{F}
-linear endomorphism
T_{\mathrm{cycl}}\mapstochar\rightarrow(1+T_{\mathrm{cycl}})^{p}-1
of
\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)
is just the endomorphism
\varphi
, the isomorphism
W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\xrightarrow[\sim]{[T_{\mathrm{cycl}}]\mapstochar\rightarrow\left[(1+T_{\mathrm{cycl}})^{\gamma}-1\right]}W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)
, which we denote by
\gamma
, decomposes as

\displaystyle\gamma:W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\xrightarrow{a}W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\xrightarrow{\varphi^{mf+d}}W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right).

(7.9)

where the first map is the action by
a\in\mathbb{Z}_{p}^{\times}
.

Since
W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\xrightarrow{[T_{\mathrm{LT}}]\mapstochar\rightarrow[T_{\mathrm{LT},0}]}W_{E}\left(A_{\infty}^{\prime}\right)
is a section of the bottom horizontal surjection of (
7.8
), using Theorem
5.11
and (
7.8
), we have

\displaystyle W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{LT},E,\sigma_{0}}}D_{\mathrm{LT},\sigma_{0}}(\rho)

\displaystyle\cong W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{W_{E}(A_{\infty}^{\prime})}W_{E}(A_{\infty}^{\prime})\otimes_{\mathrm{pr}_{0},A_{\mathrm{LT},E,\sigma_{0}}}D_{\mathrm{LT},\sigma_{0}}(\rho)

(7.10)

\displaystyle\cong W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{W_{E}(A_{\infty}^{\prime})}W_{E}(A_{\infty}^{\prime})\otimes_{W_{E}(A_{\infty})}D_{W_{E}(A_{\infty})}^{(0)}(\rho)

\displaystyle\cong W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{\iota,W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)}W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)

\displaystyle\otimes_{\mathrm{tr},W_{E}(A_{\infty})}D_{W_{E}(A_{\infty})}^{(0)}(\rho).

On the other hand, the isomorphism (
6.7
) gives

\displaystyle W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{p^{-\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{LT},E,\sigma_{0}}}D_{\mathrm{LT},\sigma_{0}}(\rho)

\displaystyle\cong W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{p^{-\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{cycl},E}}D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{0}}(\rho)

(7.11)

\displaystyle\cong W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{p^{-\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)}W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)

\displaystyle\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{cycl},E}}D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{0}}(\rho)

\displaystyle\cong W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{p^{-\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{\iota,W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)}W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)

\displaystyle\otimes_{\gamma^{-1},W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)}W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{cycl},E}}D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{0}}(\rho),

where the second isomorphism is induced by the injection (
6.3
). To simplify the notation, we put

D(\rho)\coloneq W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{\gamma^{-1},W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)}W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{cycl},E}}D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{0}}(\rho)

which is a finitely presented étale
(\varphi_{q},\mathbb{Z}_{p}^{\times})
-module over
W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)
. Combining (
7.10
) and (
7.11
), we get an isomorphism

\displaystyle W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{\iota,W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)}\left(W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{\mathrm{tr},W_{E}(A_{\infty})}D_{W_{E}(A_{\infty})}^{(0)}(\rho)\right)

(7.12)

\displaystyle\cong

\displaystyle W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{LT}}^{p^{-\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{\iota,W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)}D(\rho).

Since the actions of
\mathrm{Gal}(K_{\infty}/K(\mu_{p^{\infty}}))
on
W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{\mathrm{tr},W_{E}(A_{\infty})}D_{W_{E}(A_{\infty})}^{(0)}(\rho)
and
D(\rho)
are trivial, by taking
\mathrm{Gal}(K_{\infty}/K(\mu_{p^{\infty}}))
-invariants and using the fact that (
7.9
) is an isomorphism, we deduce that (
7.12
) descends to an isomorphism:

\displaystyle W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{\mathrm{tr},W_{E}(A_{\infty})}D_{W_{E}(A_{\infty})}^{(0)}(\rho)\cong D(\rho).

(7.13)

On the other hand, using (
7.9
), we can compute
D(\rho)
explicitly:

\displaystyle D(\rho)\cong

\displaystyle W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{a^{-1},W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)}W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)

\displaystyle\quad\otimes_{\varphi^{-nf-d},W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)}W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{cycl},E}}D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{0}}(\rho)

\displaystyle\cong

\displaystyle W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{a^{-1},A_{\mathrm{cycl},E}}A_{\mathrm{cycl},E}\otimes_{\varphi^{-nf-d},A_{\mathrm{cycl},E}}D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{0}}(\rho)

\displaystyle\cong

\displaystyle W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{a^{-1},A_{\mathrm{cycl},E}}D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{d}}(\rho)

\displaystyle\cong

\displaystyle W_{E}\left(\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\mathrm{cycl}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)\right)\otimes_{A_{\mathrm{cycl},E}}D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{d}}(\rho)

where the third isomorphism uses (
6.1
) and the last isomorphism uses the fact that the endomorphism of
D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{d}}(\rho)
given by
a\in\mathbb{Z}_{p}^{\times}
is an automorphism. Therefore, (
7.13
) induces the required isomorphism (
7.5
) which finishes the proof.

Theorem 7.4

The functor
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}:\mathrm{Rep}_{\mathcal{O}_{E}}\left(\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)\right)\rightarrow\mathrm{Mod}^{\mathrm{\acute{e}t,fp}}_{\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}}(A_{\mathrm{mv},E})
is fully faithful and exact. If
\rho
is a finite free
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-representation of
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)
, then
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(\rho)
is a finite free étale
(\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-module over
A_{\mathrm{mv},E}
, and

\mathrm{rank}_{\mathcal{O}_{E}}\rho=\mathrm{rank}_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(\rho).

Proof
.

The claim that
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}
sends finite free
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-representations to finite free étale
(\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-modules over
A_{\mathrm{mv},E}
and preserves the rank follows directly from the construction. The exactness and faithfulness has been explained in the discussion after Lemma
7.1
, hence it remains to show the fullness of
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}
.
By Lemma
7.1
, it suffices to show the fullness of
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}
. Let
\rho_{1},\rho_{2}
be finite type
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-representations of
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)
, there is a commutative diagram of abelian groups (Proposition
7.3
):

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathrm{Hom}_{\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)}(\rho_{1},\rho_{2})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathrm{Hom}_{\mathrm{Mod}^{\mathrm{\acute{e}t,proj}}_{\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}}(A_{\mathrm{mv},E})}(D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{1}),D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{2}))}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathrm{Hom}_{\mathrm{Mod}^{\mathrm{\acute{e}t,proj}}_{\varphi_{q},\mathbb{Z}_{p}^{\times}}(A_{\mathrm{cycl},E})}(D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{d}}(\rho_{1}),D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{d}}(\rho_{2})).}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\sim}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{0}}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces A_{\mathrm{cycl},E}\otimes_{\mathrm{tr},A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}-}

Thus if we can show the injectivity of the right vertical map, then the fully faithfulness of
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}
follows. Let
h:D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{1})\rightarrow D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{2})
be a homomorphism of finitely presented étale
(\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-modules such that
\mathrm{id}\otimes h:D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{d}}(\rho_{1})\rightarrow D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{d}}(\rho_{2})
is the zero map.
Let
\mathfrak{p}\subset A_{\mathrm{mv},E}
be the kernel of
\mathrm{tr}:A_{\mathrm{mv},E}\twoheadrightarrow A_{\mathrm{cycl},E}
. Since
A_{\mathrm{cycl},E}
is an integral domain,
\mathfrak{p}
is a prime ideal. As
\mathrm{id}\otimes h=0
, we have

h(D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{1}))\subseteq\mathfrak{p}\cdot D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{2}).

Since
h
commutes with
\varphi_{q}
and
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{1})
is étale, we have

h(D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{1}))\subseteq\varphi_{q}^{n}(\mathfrak{p})\cdot D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{2}),\quad n\geq 1.

For any
n\geq 1
, the map
\varphi_{q}^{nf^{\prime}}:A_{\mathrm{mv},E}/\varpi\rightarrow A_{\mathrm{mv},E}/\varpi
is the
q^{nf^{\prime}}
-th power map where
f^{\prime}=[\mathbb{F}:\mathbb{F}_{q}]
, hence
\varphi_{q}^{nf^{\prime}}(\mathfrak{p})\subseteq\mathfrak{p}^{q^{nf^{\prime}}}+\varpi A_{\mathrm{mv},E}
. Thus

h(D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{1}))\subseteq\left(\mathfrak{p}^{q^{nf^{\prime}}}+\varpi A_{\mathrm{mv},E}\right)\cdot D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{2}),\quad n\geq 1.

Let
M\coloneq\bigcap_{n=1}^{\infty}\left(\mathfrak{p}^{q^{nf^{\prime}}}+\varpi A_{\mathrm{mv},E}\right)\cdot D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{2})
, we have
h(D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{1}))\subseteq M
and
\varpi D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{2})\subseteq M
. Moreover, since
\varpi D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{2})
is contained in
\left(\mathfrak{p}^{q^{nf^{\prime}}}+\varpi A_{\mathrm{mv},E}\right)\cdot D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{2})
for every
n\geq 1
, we have

\displaystyle M\big/\varpi D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{2})

\displaystyle=\bigcap_{n=1}^{\infty}\left(\left.\left(\mathfrak{p}^{q^{nf^{\prime}}}+\varpi A_{\mathrm{mv},E}\right)\cdot D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{2})\middle/\varpi D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{2})\right.\right)

\displaystyle=\bigcap_{n=1}^{\infty}\left(\overline{\mathfrak{p}}^{q^{nf^{\prime}}}\cdot\left(D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{2})/\varpi D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{2})\right)\right),

where
\overline{\mathfrak{p}}
is the image of
\mathfrak{p}
under the surjection
A_{\mathrm{mv},E}\twoheadrightarrow A
which is a proper ideal of
A
. Although
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{2})
may not be a free
A_{\mathrm{mv},E}
-module, by Proposition
4.10
,
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{2})/\varpi D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{2})
is always a finite free
A
-module. Therefore,

\displaystyle M/\varpi D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{2})

\displaystyle=\bigcap_{n=1}^{\infty}\left(\overline{\mathfrak{p}}^{q^{nf^{\prime}}}\cdot\left(D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{2})/\varpi D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{2})\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\bigcap_{n=1}^{\infty}\overline{\mathfrak{p}}^{q^{nf^{\prime}}}\right)\cdot\left(D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{2})/\varpi D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{2})\right).

As
\overline{\mathfrak{p}}\subsetneq A
is a proper ideal and
A
is a Noetherian domain, by Krull’s intersection theorem,
\bigcap_{n=1}^{\infty}\overline{\mathfrak{p}}^{q^{nf^{\prime}}}=0
, thus

M=\varpi D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{2}),

which implies that

\displaystyle h(D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{1}))\subseteq\varpi D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{2})=D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\varpi\rho_{2}).

(7.14)

By induction on
m\geq 1
, one can show that

h(D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{1}))\subseteq D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\varpi^{m}\rho_{2})=\varpi^{m}D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{2}),\quad m\geq 1

hence

h(D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{1}))\subseteq\bigcap_{m=1}^{\infty}\varpi^{m}\cdot D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{2}).

By
[
Sta24
,
Tag 00IQ
]
, as
\varpi
is contained in the Jacobson radical of
A_{\mathrm{mv},E}
and
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{2})
is a finite
A_{\mathrm{mv},E}
-module, we have

\bigcap_{m=1}^{\infty}\varpi^{m}\cdot D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(0)}(\rho_{2})=0.

Therefore,
h
is the zero map, which finishes the proof.

Remark 7.5

If
K=\mathbb{Q}_{p}
, then
A_{\mathrm{mv},E}
is nothing but
A_{\mathrm{cycl},E}=A_{\mathrm{LT},E,\sigma_{0}}
, and the functor
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}
is
D_{\mathrm{cycl},\sigma_{0}}=D_{\mathrm{LT},\sigma_{0}}=D_{\mathrm{LT}}
, hence is an equivalence of categories. If
K\neq\mathbb{Q}_{p}
, as the following proposition shows, the functor
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}
is not essentially surjective.

Proposition 7.6

For
0\leq i\leq f-1
, the functor
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}:\mathrm{Rep}_{\mathcal{O}_{E}}\left(\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)\right)\rightarrow\mathrm{Mod}^{\mathrm{\acute{e}t,fp}}_{\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}}(A_{\mathrm{mv},E})
is not essentially surjective if
f\geq 2
.

Proof
.

By Corollary
4.19
, it is equivalent to show that the functor
D_{W_{E}(A_{\infty})}^{(i)}
is not essentially surjective.

For finitely presented étale
(\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-modules
M_{1},M_{2}
over
W_{E}(A_{\infty})
, we set

\mathrm{Ext}^{1}(M_{1},M_{2})\coloneq\mathrm{Ext}^{1}_{\mathrm{Mod}^{\mathrm{\acute{e}t,fp}}_{\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}}(W_{E}(A_{\infty}))}(M_{1},M_{2}),

which is an
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-module. Similarly, for finite type continuous
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-representations
\rho_{1},\rho_{2}
of
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)
, we set
\mathrm{Ext}^{1}(\rho_{1},\rho_{2})\coloneq\mathrm{Ext}^{1}_{\mathrm{Rep}_{\mathcal{O}_{E}}(\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K))}(\rho_{1},\rho_{2})
which is also an
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-module. Viewing
\mathcal{O}_{E}
as a trivial representation of
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)
, we have
D_{W_{E}(A_{\infty})}^{(i)}(\mathcal{O}_{E})=W_{E}(A_{\infty})
where
W_{E}(A_{\infty})
is regarded as a trivial étale
(\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-module. Theorem
7.4
and Corollary
4.19
imply that the functor
D_{W_{E}(A_{\infty})}^{(i)}
is fully faithful and exact, hence induces an injection of
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-modules

\displaystyle\mathrm{Ext}^{1}(\mathcal{O}_{E},\mathcal{O}_{E})\hookrightarrow\mathrm{Ext}^{1}\left(W_{E}(A_{\infty}),W_{E}(A_{\infty})\right).

(7.15)

We will show that (
7.15
) is not surjective, hence
D_{W_{E}(A_{\infty})}^{(i)}
cannot be essentially surjective.

Suppose that
0\rightarrow W_{E}(A_{\infty})\xrightarrow{f}V\xrightarrow{g}W_{E}(A_{\infty})\rightarrow 0
is an extension of
W_{E}(A_{\infty})
by itself in
{\mathrm{Mod}^{\mathrm{\acute{e}t,fp}}_{\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}}(W_{E}(A_{\infty}))}
. Let
e_{0}\coloneq f(1)
, and take
e_{1}\in g^{-1}(1)
, we have
V=W_{E}(A_{\infty})e_{0}\oplus W_{E}(A_{\infty})e_{1}
, and there exists
x\in W_{E}(A_{\infty})
such that
\varphi_{q}(e_{1})=xe_{0}+e_{1}
. Moreover, for any
a\in\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}
, there exists
c_{a}\in W_{E}(A_{\infty})
such that
a(e_{1})=c_{a}e_{0}+e_{1}
.
Since
\varphi_{q}
commutes with the action of
\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}
, we get

\displaystyle\begin{cases}a(x)-x=\varphi_{q}(c_{a})-c_{a},&a\in\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}\\
c_{a_{1}a_{2}}=a_{1}(c_{a_{2}})+c_{a_{2}},&a_{1},a_{2}\in\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}.\end{cases}

(7.16)

Let
Z^{1}(W_{E}(A_{\infty}))
be the
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-module
\left\{(x,c)\in W_{E}(A_{\infty})\oplus Z^{1}_{\mathrm{cont}}(\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times},W_{E}(A_{\infty})):(x,c)\ \text{satisfies (\ref{equivalent description of extensions of W_E(A_{infty}) by itself})}\right\}
where
Z^{1}_{\mathrm{cont}}(\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times},W_{E}(A_{\infty}))
is the abelian group of continuous
1
-cocycles. Let
d
be the homomorphism of
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-modules
W_{E}(A_{\infty})\rightarrow Z^{1}(W_{E}(A_{\infty})),\ \alpha\mapstochar\rightarrow\big(\varphi_{q}(\alpha)-\alpha,a\mapstochar\rightarrow(a(\alpha)-\alpha)\big)
,
and let
H^{1}(W_{E}(A_{\infty}))
be the quotient
Z^{1}(W_{E}(A_{\infty}))/\mathrm{Im}d
. It is straightforward to verify that the map

\displaystyle h:\mathrm{Ext}^{1}(W_{E}(A_{\infty}),W_{E}(A_{\infty}))\rightarrow H^{1}(W_{E}(A_{\infty})),\ V\mapstochar\rightarrow[(x,c)]

(7.17)

is well-defined and is an isomorphism of
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-modules. Hence we identify
\mathrm{Ext}^{1}(W_{E}(A_{\infty}),W_{E}(A_{\infty}))
with
H^{1}(W_{E}(A_{\infty}))
. Similarly, there is an isomorphism of
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-modules

\mathrm{Ext}^{1}(\mathcal{O}_{E},\mathcal{O}_{E})\cong H^{1}_{\mathrm{cont}}(\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K),\mathcal{O}_{E})\cong\mathcal{O}_{E}^{f+1}.

where the last isomorphism uses the fact that
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)
acts trivially on
\mathcal{O}_{E}
.

Now we prove that
H^{1}(W_{E}(A_{\infty}))
is not a finite type
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-module. First, we claim that
H^{1}(W_{E}(A_{\infty}))
is torsion-free as an
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-module. For
[(x,c)]\in H^{1}(W_{E}(A_{\infty}))
satisfies
\varpi\cdot[(x,c)]=0
, we fix a representative
(x,c)\in Z^{1}(W_{E}(A_{\infty}))
, then there exists
\alpha\in W_{E}(A_{\infty})
such that
\varpi\cdot(x,c)=d(\alpha)
. In particular,
\varphi_{q}(\alpha)-\alpha=\varpi x\in\varpi\cdot W_{E}(A_{\infty})
. Write
\alpha=\sum_{i=0}^{+\infty}[\alpha_{i}]\varpi^{i}
,
\alpha_{i}\in A_{\infty}
, then
\varphi_{q}(\alpha_{0})=\alpha_{0}
,
which easily implies that
\alpha_{0}\in\mathbb{F}
. Write
\alpha-[\alpha_{0}]=\varpi\beta
for some
\beta\in W_{E}(A_{\infty})
, we have
(x,c)=d(\beta)
, hence
[(x,c)]=0
, which proves the claim.

For
k\geq 1
, let
x_{k}\coloneq[T_{\sigma_{0}}^{-(q-1)k}]\cdot[T_{\sigma_{1}}^{(q-1)k}]\in W_{E}(A_{\infty})
be the Teichmüller lift of
T_{\sigma_{0}}^{-(q-1)k}\cdot T_{\sigma_{1}}^{(q-1)k}\in A_{\infty}
. For
a\in\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}
, applying (
2.6
), we have

\displaystyle\left|a\left(\dfrac{T_{\sigma_{1}}}{T_{\sigma_{0}}}\right)-\dfrac{\sigma_{1}(\overline{a})T_{\sigma_{1}}}{\sigma_{0}(\overline{a})T_{\sigma_{0}}}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\dfrac{a(T_{\sigma_{1}})\Big(\sigma_{0}(\overline{a})T_{\sigma_{0}}-a(T_{\sigma_{0}})\Big)+a(T_{\sigma_{0}})\Big(a(T_{\sigma_{1}})-\sigma_{1}(\overline{a})T_{\sigma_{1}}\Big)}{a(T_{\sigma_{0}})\sigma_{0}(\overline{a})T_{\sigma_{0}}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{\max\left(\left|a(T_{\sigma_{1}})\Big(\sigma_{0}(\overline{a})T_{\sigma_{0}}-a(T_{\sigma_{0}})\Big)\right|,\left|a(T_{\sigma_{0}})\Big(a(T_{\sigma_{1}})-\sigma_{1}(\overline{a})T_{\sigma_{1}}\Big)\right|\right)}{\left|T_{\sigma_{0}}\right|^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq|T_{\sigma_{0}}|^{p-1},

where
|\cdot|:A_{\infty}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}
is the multiplicative norm. This implies that

\displaystyle\left|a\left(\dfrac{T_{\sigma_{1}}}{T_{\sigma_{0}}}\right)^{(q-1)k}-\left(\dfrac{T_{\sigma_{1}}}{T_{\sigma_{0}}}\right)^{(q-1)k}\right|\leq|T_{\sigma_{0}}|^{p-1},\quad k\geq 1.

(7.18)

The construction of ramified Witt vectors implies that there exists
P_{n}(X,Y)\in\mathbb{F}[X,Y]
,
n\geq 0
, such that

\displaystyle a(x_{k})-x_{k}=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\varpi^{n}\left[a\left(\dfrac{T_{\sigma_{1}}}{T_{\sigma_{0}}}\right)^{(q-1)k}-\left(\dfrac{T_{\sigma_{1}}}{T_{\sigma_{0}}}\right)^{(q-1)k}\right]^{{q^{\prime}}^{-n}}\cdot\left[P_{n}\left(a\left(\dfrac{T_{\sigma_{1}}}{T_{\sigma_{0}}}\right)^{(q-1)k},\left(\dfrac{T_{\sigma_{1}}}{T_{\sigma_{0}}}\right)^{(q-1)k}\right)\right]^{{q^{\prime}}^{-n}}.

For
m\geq 0
, using (
7.18
), we have

\displaystyle N_{m}\left(a(x_{k})-x_{k}\right)

\displaystyle=\sup_{0\leq n\leq m}\left|a\left(\dfrac{T_{\sigma_{1}}}{T_{\sigma_{0}}}\right)^{(q-1)k}-\left(\dfrac{T_{\sigma_{1}}}{T_{\sigma_{0}}}\right)^{(q-1)k}\right|^{{q^{\prime}}^{-n}}\cdot\left|P_{n}\left(a\left(\dfrac{T_{\sigma_{1}}}{T_{\sigma_{0}}}\right)^{(q-1)k},\left(\dfrac{T_{\sigma_{1}}}{T_{\sigma_{0}}}\right)^{(q-1)k}\right)\right|^{{q^{\prime}}^{-n}}

\displaystyle\leq|T_{\sigma_{0}}|^{{q^{\prime}}^{-m}(p-1)},

where
N_{m}
is defined in Lemma
3.5
.
Therefore, for
n\geq 1
,
a\in\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}
,

\displaystyle N_{m}\left(\varphi_{q}^{n}(a(x_{k})-x_{k})\right)=N_{m}\left(a(x_{k})-x_{k}\right)^{q^{n}}\leq|T_{\sigma_{0}}|^{q^{n}{q^{\prime}}^{-m}(p-1)}.

This implies that
\lim\limits_{n\rightarrow+\infty}\varphi_{q}^{n}(a(x_{k})-x_{k})=0
, hence
c_{k,a}\coloneq\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}\varphi_{q}^{n}(a(x_{k})-x_{k})\in W_{E}(A_{\infty})
exists, and the map
c_{k}:\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}\rightarrow W_{E}(A_{\infty})
,
a\mapstochar\rightarrow c_{k,a}
is continuous for
k\geq 1
. Moreover, it is easy to check that
(x_{k},c_{k})
satisfies (
7.16
), hence represents an element
[(x_{k},c_{k})]\in H^{1}(W_{E}(A_{\infty}))
for
k\geq 1
.

Let
S\coloneq\left\{k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 1}:p\nmid k\right\}
be the set of positive integers which are not divisible by
p
.
Let
S_{1}
be a finite subset of
S
. Suppose that
\left\{[(x_{k},c_{k})],k\in S_{1}\right\}
are
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-linearly dependent in
H^{1}(W_{E}(A_{\infty}))
. Since
H^{1}(W_{E}(A_{\infty}))
is torsion-free, we may assume that there exists
a_{k}\in\mathcal{O}_{E},k\in S_{1}
,
a_{k_{0}}\notin\varpi\mathcal{O}_{E}
for some
k_{0}\in S_{1}
and
\alpha\in W_{E}(A_{\infty})
such that

\sum_{i\in S_{1}}a_{k}\cdot(x_{k},c_{k})=d(\alpha)\in Z^{1}(W_{E}(A_{\infty})).

In particular, by reducing modulo
\varpi
, we have

\sum_{k\in S_{1}}\overline{a}_{k}\overline{x}_{k}=\sum_{k\in S_{1}}\overline{a}_{k}\left(\dfrac{T_{\sigma_{k}}}{T_{\sigma_{0}}}\right)^{(q-1)k}=\varphi_{q}(\overline{\alpha})-\overline{\alpha}\in A_{\infty}

with
\overline{a}_{k}\in\mathbb{F}
and
\overline{a}_{k_{0}}\neq 0
for some
k_{0}\in S_{1}
. Write

\overline{\alpha}=\sum_{\underline{n}\in\left(\mathbb{Z}[1/p]\right)^{f-1}}f_{\underline{n}}(T_{\sigma_{0}})\prod_{j=1}^{f-1}\left(\dfrac{T_{\sigma_{j}}}{T_{\sigma_{0}}}\right)^{n_{j}}\in A_{\infty}

with
f_{\underline{n}}(T_{\sigma_{0}})\in\mathbb{F}(\negthinspace(T_{\sigma_{0}}^{1/p^{\infty}})\negthinspace)
satisfying that for any
l\geq 1
, there is only finitely many
\underline{n}\in\left(\mathbb{Z}[1/p]\right)^{f-1}
such that
f_{\underline{n}}(T_{\sigma_{0}})\notin T_{\sigma_{0}}^{l}\mathbb{F}[\negthinspace[T_{\sigma_{0}}^{1/p^{\infty}}]\negthinspace]
. In particular, let
n_{0}\coloneq((q-1)k_{0},0,\dots,0)\in\mathbb{Z}^{f-1}
, we have

\lim_{m\rightarrow+\infty}|f_{\frac{1}{q^{m}}\underline{n_{0}}}(T_{\sigma_{0}})|=\lim_{m\rightarrow+\infty}|f_{{q^{m}}\underline{n_{0}}}(T_{\sigma_{0}})|=0.

Note that

\varphi_{q}(\overline{\alpha})-\overline{\alpha}=\sum_{\underline{n}\in\left(\mathbb{Z}[1/p]\right)^{f-1}}\left(\varphi_{q}(f_{\frac{1}{q}\underline{n}}(T_{\sigma_{0}}))-f_{\underline{n}}(T_{\sigma_{0}})\right)\prod_{j=1}^{f-1}\left(\dfrac{T_{\sigma_{j}}}{T_{\sigma_{0}}}\right)^{n_{j}}.

By our choice for
S
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\varphi_{q}(f_{\frac{1}{q}\underline{n}_{0}}(T_{\sigma_{0}}))-f_{\underline{n}_{0}}(T_{\sigma_{0}})=\overline{a}_{k_{0}}\neq 0,&\\
\varphi_{q}(f_{\frac{1}{q^{m}}\underline{n}_{0}}(T_{\sigma_{0}}))-f_{\frac{1}{q^{m-1}}\underline{n}_{0}}(T_{\sigma_{0}})=0,&m\in\mathbb{Z},m\neq 1.\end{cases}

(7.19)

If
-f_{\underline{n}_{0}}(T_{\sigma_{0}})=\overline{a}_{k_{0}}\in\mathbb{F}^{\times}
, then (
7.19
) implies

f_{q^{m}\underline{n}_{0}}(T_{\sigma_{0}})=\varphi_{q}(f_{q^{m-1}\underline{n}_{0}}(T_{\sigma_{0}}))=\cdots=\varphi_{q}^{m}(f_{\underline{n_{0}}}(T_{\sigma_{0}}))=\overline{a}_{k_{0}}\neq 0,\quad m\geq 1,

hence
\lim\limits_{m\rightarrow+\infty}|f_{{q^{m}}\underline{n_{0}}}(T_{\sigma_{0}})|=1
which is impossible. Hence
-f_{\underline{n}_{0}}(T_{\sigma_{0}})\neq\overline{a}_{k_{0}}
, i.e.
|f_{\underline{n}_{0}}(T_{\sigma_{0}})+\overline{a}_{k_{0}}|\neq 0
, and for
m\geq 1
, using (
7.19
) and the fact that
|\varphi_{q}(x)|=|x|^{q}
for any
x\in A_{\infty}
, we have

\displaystyle|f_{\frac{1}{q^{m}}\underline{n}_{0}}(T_{\sigma_{0}})|=|\varphi_{q}(f_{\frac{1}{q^{m}}\underline{n}_{0}})(T_{\sigma_{0}})|^{q^{-1}}

\displaystyle=|f_{\frac{1}{q^{m-1}}\underline{n}_{0}}(T_{\sigma_{0}})|^{q^{-1}}=\cdots

\displaystyle=\left|f_{\frac{1}{q}\underline{n}_{0}}(T_{\sigma_{0}})\right|^{q^{-(m-1)}}=\left|\varphi_{q}(f_{\frac{1}{q}\underline{n}_{0}}(T_{\sigma_{0}}))\right|^{q^{-m}}

\displaystyle=|\overline{a}_{k_{0}}+f_{\underline{n}_{0}}(T_{\sigma_{0}})|^{q^{-m}},

thus

\lim_{m\rightarrow+\infty}|f_{\frac{1}{q^{m}}\underline{n_{0}}}(T_{\sigma_{0}})|=\lim_{m\rightarrow+\infty}|\overline{a}_{k_{0}}+f_{\underline{n}_{0}}(T_{\sigma_{0}})|^{q^{-m}}=1

which is also impossible. Therefore, for any finite subset
S_{1}\subset S
,
\left\{[(x_{k},c_{k})]:k\in S_{1}\right\}
are linearly independent, thus the
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-submodule of
H^{1}(W_{E}(A_{\infty}))
generated by
\left\{[(x_{k},c_{k})]:k\in S\right\}
is not finitely generated, hence
H^{1}(W_{E}(A_{\infty}))
is not finitely generated, thus the inclusion (
7.15
) is not surjective. Therefore,
D_{W_{E}(A_{\infty})}^{(i)}
is not essentially surjective.

7.2
Finitely presented étale
(\varphi,\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-modules attached to finite type continuous
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-representations of
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)

As in
[
BHHMS23a
,
\lx@sectionsign
2.7]
, one can also define the functor
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{\otimes}
from the category of finite type
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-representations to the category of finitely presented étale
(\varphi,\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-modules over
A_{\mathrm{mv},E}
.

Recall that the multiplication by
p
on
\mathcal{O}_{K}\cong N_{0}
induces a continuous endomorphism of
\mathcal{O}_{K}[\negthinspace[N_{0}]\negthinspace]
which extends continuously and uniquely to an endomorphism
\varphi
of
A_{\mathrm{mv},K}
. We put
\varphi\coloneq\mathrm{id}\otimes\varphi:A_{\mathrm{mv},E}=\mathcal{O}_{E}\otimes_{\sigma_{0},\mathcal{O}_{K}}A_{\mathrm{mv},K}\rightarrow A_{\mathrm{mv},E}
, then
\varphi^{f}=\varphi_{q}
, and
\varphi
commutes with the action of
\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}
.
Using the same arguments as in the proof of Lemma
2.3
, we can show that the ring map
\varphi:A_{\mathrm{mv},E}\rightarrow A_{\mathrm{mv},E}
is also finitely presented and faithfully flat.

For a finite type
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-representation
\rho
of
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)
, we put

\displaystyle D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{\otimes}(\rho)\coloneq\bigotimes_{i=0}^{f-1}D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(\rho).

(7.20)

Let
\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}
acts on
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{\otimes}(\rho)
diagonally and we define the endomorphism
\varphi
of
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{\otimes}(\rho)
:

\varphi(x_{0}\otimes\cdots\otimes x_{f-1})\coloneq h_{f-1}(x_{f-1})\otimes h_{0}(x_{0})\otimes\cdots\otimes h_{f-2}(x_{f-2}),

where
h_{i}
is the composition for
0\leq i\leq f-1

h_{i}:D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(\rho){\mathrel{\rightarrow}^{x\mapstochar\rightarrow 1\otimes x}}A_{\mathrm{mv},E}\otimes_{\varphi,A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(\rho)\xrightarrow[\sim]{\phi_{i}}D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i+1)}(\rho).

For the definition of
\phi_{i}
, see Lemma
7.1
.

Proposition 7.7

For any finite type
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-representation
\rho
of
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)
,
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{\otimes}(\rho)
is a finitely presented étale
(\varphi,\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-module over
A_{\mathrm{mv},E}
.

Proof
.

Clearly,
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{\otimes}(\rho)
is a finite
A_{\mathrm{mv},E}
-module with a semi-linear
(\varphi,\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-action. Note that the linearization map

A_{\mathrm{mv},E}\otimes_{\varphi}D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{\otimes}(\rho)\xrightarrow{\mathrm{id}\otimes\varphi}D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{\otimes}(\rho)

decomposes as

\displaystyle A_{\mathrm{mv},E}\otimes_{\varphi}D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{\otimes}(\rho)

\displaystyle=A_{\mathrm{mv},E}\otimes_{\varphi}\bigotimes_{i=0}^{f-1}D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(\rho)

\displaystyle\cong\bigotimes_{i=0}^{f-1}\left(A_{\mathrm{mv},E}\otimes_{\varphi}D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(\rho)\right)

\displaystyle\cong\bigotimes_{i=0}^{f-1}D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i+1)}(\rho)

\displaystyle\cong\bigotimes_{i=0}^{f-1}D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(\rho)=D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{\otimes}(\rho),

where the third isomorphism uses (
7.3
), thus
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{\otimes}(\rho)
is étale.

Therefore, we obtain a functor
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{\otimes}:\mathrm{Rep}_{\mathcal{O}_{E}}(\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K))\rightarrow\mathrm{Mod}^{\mathrm{\acute{e}t,fp}}_{\varphi,\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}}(A_{\mathrm{mv},E})
. The functor
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{\otimes}
is neither full nor additive if
f\geq 2
, but might be related to
p
-adic representations of
\mathrm{GL}_{2}(K)
(see
[
BHHMS23
, Corollary 3.1.4]
and
[
Wan24
, Theorem 1.1]
for mod
p
representations).

7.3
Finite free étale
(\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-modules attached to finite dimensional continuous
E
-representations of
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)

In this section, for
0\leq i\leq f-1
, we associate to every finite dimensional
E
-representation of
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)
a finite free étale
(\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-module over
A_{\mathrm{mv},E}\left[\dfrac{1}{p}\right]
, and show that this construction gives a fully faithful exact functor.

Let
B_{\mathrm{mv},E}\coloneq A_{\mathrm{mv},E}\left[\dfrac{1}{p}\right]
. We endow
B_{\mathrm{mv},E}=\bigcup_{n=1}p^{-n}A_{\mathrm{mv},E}
with the colimit topology, then the continuous
(\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-action on
A_{\mathrm{mv},E}
extends to a continuous
(\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-action on
B_{\mathrm{mv},E}
.
For a finite
B_{\mathrm{mv},E}
-module
M
, we can choose a surjection
B_{\mathrm{mv},E}^{n}\twoheadrightarrow M
of
B_{\mathrm{mv},E}
-modules, and then we endow
M
with the quotient topology, where
B_{\mathrm{mv},E}^{n}
is equipped with the product topology. It is easy to check that the topology on
M
does not depend on the choice of the surjection
B_{\mathrm{mv},E}^{n}\twoheadrightarrow M
.

Definition 7.8

Let
M
be a finite free module over
B_{\mathrm{mv},E}
with a continuous semi-linear endomorphism
\varphi_{q}
and a continuous semi-linear action of
\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}
, we say that
M
is a
finite free étale

(\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-
module
over
B_{\mathrm{mv},E}
, if there is a
B_{\mathrm{mv},E}
-basis
e_{1},\dots,e_{r}
of
M
such that
\oplus_{i=1}^{r}A_{\mathrm{mv},E}e_{i}
is an étale
(\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-module over
A_{\mathrm{mv},E}
. We denote by
\mathrm{Mod}^{\mathrm{\acute{e}t,proj}}_{\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}}(B_{\mathrm{mv},E})
the category of finite free étale
(\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-modules over
B_{\mathrm{mv},E}
.

From Proposition
4.10
, Lemma
4.11
, and the definition, we deduce:

Proposition 7.9

The category
\mathrm{Mod}^{\mathrm{\acute{e}t,proj}}_{\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}}(B_{\mathrm{mv},E})
is an abelian category.

Let
\mathrm{Rep}_{E}(\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K))
be the category of finite dimensional continuous
E
-representations of
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)
. For any object
\rho
in
\mathrm{Rep}_{E}(\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K))
, recall that there exists an open finite
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-submodule
\rho_{0}
of
\rho
which is stable under the action of
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)
. Such
\rho_{0}
is called an
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-
lattice
of
\rho
.

For any finite dimensional
E
-representation
\rho
of
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)
,
0\leq i\leq f-1
, we choose an
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-lattice
\rho_{0}
of
\rho
and put

D_{B_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(\rho)\coloneq D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(\rho_{0})\left[\frac{1}{p}\right].

It is easy to check that
D_{B_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(\rho)
does not depend on the choice of the
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-lattice
\rho_{0}
. Furthermore, for any homomorphism
f:\rho\rightarrow\rho^{\prime}
of finite dimensional continuous
E
-representations of
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)
, we can take
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-lattices
\rho_{0}
and
\rho_{0}^{\prime}
of
\rho
and
\rho^{\prime}
respectively such that
f(\rho_{0})\subseteq\rho_{0}^{\prime}
, hence there is a homomorphism
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(f|_{\rho_{0}}):D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(\rho_{0})\rightarrow D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(\rho_{0}^{\prime})
of finite free étale
(\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-modules over
A_{\mathrm{mv},E}
, and inverting
p
induces a homomorphism of finite free étale
(\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-modules over
B_{\mathrm{mv},E}
:

D_{B_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(f):D_{B_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(\rho)\rightarrow D_{B_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(\rho^{\prime}).

We can easily check that
D_{B_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(f)
is also well-defined. Thus we obtain a functor

\displaystyle D_{B_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}:\mathrm{Rep}_{E}(\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K))

\displaystyle\rightarrow\mathrm{Mod}^{\mathrm{\acute{e}t,proj}}_{\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}}(B_{\mathrm{mv},E}),

(7.21)

\displaystyle\rho

\displaystyle\mapstochar\rightarrow D_{B_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(\rho).

Theorem 7.10

Let
0\leq i\leq f-1
. The functor
D_{B_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}
is exact, fully faithful and preserves the rank, i.e. for
\rho\in\mathrm{Rep}_{E}(\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K))
, we have

\dim_{E}\rho=\mathrm{rank}_{B_{\mathrm{mv},E}}D_{B_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(\rho).

Proof
.

Let
0\rightarrow\rho^{\prime}\xrightarrow{f}\rho\xrightarrow{g}\rho^{\prime\prime}\rightarrow 0
be a short exact sequence in
\mathrm{Rep}_{E}(\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K))
, and
\rho_{0}
be an
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-lattice of
\rho
, then
f^{-1}(\rho_{0})
and
g(\rho_{0})
are
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-lattices of
\rho^{\prime}
and
\rho^{\prime\prime}
respectively. By Theorem
7.4
, the sequence

0\rightarrow D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(f^{-1}(\rho_{0}))\rightarrow D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(\rho_{0})\rightarrow D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(g(\rho_{0}))\rightarrow 0

is exact. Inverting
p
, we deduce that
D_{B_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}
is an exact functor.

Let
\rho
,
\rho^{\prime}
be finite dimensional continuous
E
-representations of
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)
, and let
\rho_{0}
,
\rho_{0}^{\prime}
be
\mathcal{O}_{E}
-lattices of
\rho
,
\rho^{\prime}
respectively. If
f:\rho\rightarrow\rho^{\prime}
is a homomorphism of representations such that
D_{B_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(f)=0
, we may assume that
f(\rho_{0})\subseteq\rho_{0}^{\prime}
, then
D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(f|_{\rho_{0}})=0
, and by Theorem
7.4
,
f|_{\rho_{0}}:\rho_{0}\rightarrow\rho_{0}^{\prime}
is the zero map, hence
f=0
. Conversely, let
g:D_{B_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(\rho)\rightarrow D_{B_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(\rho^{\prime})
be a homomorphism of finite free étale
(\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-modules, replacing
\rho_{0}
by
\varpi^{n}\rho_{0}
for some
n\geq 1
, we may assume that
g\left(D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(\rho_{0})\right)\subseteq D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(\rho_{0}^{\prime})
, hence
g|_{D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(\rho_{0})}=D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(h_{0})
for the unique
h_{0}:\rho_{0}\rightarrow\rho_{0}^{\prime}
by Theorem
7.4
. Then let
h:\rho\rightarrow\rho^{\prime}
be the extension of
h_{0}
, we have
D_{B_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(h)=g
. Therefore,
D_{B_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}
is fully faithful. The last assertion comes from the following equality:

\dim_{E}\rho=\mathrm{rank}_{\mathcal{O}_{E}}\rho_{0}=\mathrm{rank}_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}(D_{A_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(\rho_{0}))=\mathrm{rank}_{{}_{\mathrm{mv},E}}(D_{B_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(\rho)).

Proposition 7.11

Let
f\geq 2
. The functor
D_{B_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}
is not essentially surjective.

Proof
.

Let
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)
acts trivially on
E
, then
D_{B_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}(E)=B_{\mathrm{mv},E}
.
Using an analogue of (
7.17
), we have

\mathrm{Ext}_{\mathrm{Mod}^{\mathrm{\acute{e}t,fp}}_{\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}}(A_{\mathrm{mv},E})}^{1}(A_{\mathrm{mv},E},A_{\mathrm{mv},E})\left[\frac{1}{p}\right]\cong\bigcup_{n=0}^{+\infty}\mathrm{Ext}_{\mathrm{Mod}^{\mathrm{\acute{e}t,fp}}_{\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}}(A_{\mathrm{mv},E})}^{1}(A_{\mathrm{mv},E},p^{-n}A_{\mathrm{mv},E}).

Let
0\rightarrow p^{-n}A_{\mathrm{mv},E}\xrightarrow{f_{0}}M_{0}\xrightarrow{g_{0}}A_{\mathrm{mv},E}\rightarrow 0
be an extension in
\mathrm{Mod}^{\mathrm{\acute{e}t,fp}}_{\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}}(A_{\mathrm{mv},E})
. If there exists a section
s:B_{\mathrm{mv},E}\rightarrow M_{0}[1/p]
of
g_{0}:M_{0}[1/p]\rightarrow B_{\mathrm{mv},E}
, then
s
restricts to a section of
g_{0}:M_{0}\rightarrow A_{\mathrm{mv},E}
, hence the extension
0\rightarrow p^{-n}A_{\mathrm{mv},E}\xrightarrow{f_{0}}M_{0}\xrightarrow{g_{0}}A_{\mathrm{mv},E}\rightarrow 0
splits. Thus the natural map
\mathrm{Ext}_{\mathrm{Mod}^{\mathrm{\acute{e}t,fp}}_{\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}}(A_{\mathrm{mv},E})}^{1}(A_{\mathrm{mv},E},p^{-n}A_{\mathrm{mv},E})\rightarrow\mathrm{Ext}^{1}_{\mathrm{Mod}^{\mathrm{\acute{e}t,proj}}_{\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}}(B_{\mathrm{mv},E})}(B_{\mathrm{mv},E},B_{\mathrm{mv},E})
is injective for every
n\geq 0
. Therefore, the natural map

\mathrm{Ext}_{\mathrm{Mod}^{\mathrm{\acute{e}t,fp}}_{\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}}(A_{\mathrm{mv},E})}^{1}(A_{\mathrm{mv},E},A_{\mathrm{mv},E})\left[\frac{1}{p}\right]\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\meno}$}}}\mathrel{\mkern-3.0mu}\rightarrow\mathrm{Ext}^{1}_{\mathrm{Mod}^{\mathrm{\acute{e}t,proj}}_{\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}}(B_{\mathrm{mv},E})}(B_{\mathrm{mv},E},B_{\mathrm{mv},E})

is injective. By the proof of Proposition
7.6
,
\mathrm{Ext}^{1}_{\mathrm{Mod}^{\mathrm{\acute{e}t,proj}}_{\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}}(B_{\mathrm{mv},E})}(B_{\mathrm{mv},E},B_{\mathrm{mv},E})
is an infinite dimensional vector space over
E
. On the other hand, there is a natural isomorphism

\mathrm{Ext}^{1}_{\mathrm{Rep}_{E}(\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K},K))}(E,E)\cong H^{1}_{\mathrm{cont}}(\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K),E)\cong E^{f+1},

thus the natural injection

\mathrm{Ext}^{1}_{\mathrm{Rep}_{E}(\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K},K))}(E,E)\hookrightarrow\mathrm{Ext}^{1}_{\mathrm{Mod}^{\mathrm{\acute{e}t,proj}}_{\varphi_{q},\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times}}(B_{\mathrm{mv},E})}(B_{\mathrm{mv},E},B_{\mathrm{mv},E})

induced by the exact fully faithful functor
D_{B_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}
is not surjective. Therefore,
D_{B_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{(i)}
is not essentially surjective.

For any finite dimensional
E
-representation
\rho
of
\mathrm{Gal}(\overline{K}/K)
, similar to (
7.20
), we define
D_{B_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{\otimes}(\rho)\coloneq\bigotimes_{i=0}^{f-1}D_{B_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{i}(\rho)
. By an argument similar to the proof of Proposition
7.7
,
D_{B_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{\otimes}(\rho)
is a finite type étale
(\varphi,\mathcal{O}_{K}^{\times})
-module over
B_{\mathrm{mv},E}
. When
\rho
is the restriction of an automorphic Galois representation, we expect
D_{B_{\mathrm{mv},E}}^{\otimes}(\rho)
to be related to
p
-adic unitary Banach representations of
\mathrm{GL}_{2}(K)
over
E
appearing in the completed cohomology of Shimura curves (see
[
BHHMS23
, Corollary 3.1.4]
and
[
Wan24
, Theorem 1.1]
for mod
p
representations).
